package LinerList;
public interface ListInterface<T> { 
  void Init(int initsize);//ᤒ
  int length();
  boolean isEmpty();//ԅᑮ
  int ElemIndex(T t);//ݩ
  T getElem(int index) throws Exception;//໑ഝindexහഝ
  void add(int index,T t) throws Exception;//໑ഝindexහഝ
  void delete(int index) throws Exception;
  void add(T t) throws Exception;//ف
  void set(int index,T t) throws Exception;
  String toString();//᫨౮Stringڊ  
}

package LinerList;
public class seqlist<T> implements ListInterface<T> {
  private Object[] date;//හᕟਂනහഝ
  private int lenth;
  public seqlist() {//তय़ੜἕᦊԅ10
    Init(10);
  }
  public void Init(int initsize) {//۸
    this.date=new Object[initsize];
    lenth=0;    
  }
  public int length() {   
    return this.lenth;
  }
  public boolean isEmpty() {//ԅᑮ
    if(this.lenth==0)
      return true;
    return false;
  }
  /*
   * * @param t 
   * ፘᒵᕮຎ҅ԅ-1ԅfalse
   */
  public int ElemIndex(T t) {

=== PDF PAGE 8 ===
 
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    for(int i=0;i<date.length;i++)
    {
      if(date[i].equals(t))
      {
        return i;
      }
    }
    return -1;
  }
  /*
   *ᔰ
   */
  public T getElem(int index) throws Exception {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    if(index<0||index>lenth-1)
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    return (T) date[index];
  }
  
  public void add(T t) throws Exception {//ف
     add(lenth,t);
  }
  /*
   *ف
   /*
  public void add(int index, T t) throws Exception {
    if(index<0||index>lenth)
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    if (lenth==date.length)//ಘ਻
    {
      Object newdate[]= new Object[lenth*2];
      for(int i=0;i<lenth;i++)
      {
        newdate[i]=date[i];
      }
      date=newdate;
    }
    for(int i=lenth-1;i>=index;i--)//ۖ
    {
      date[i+1]=date[i];
    }
    date[index]=t;//ᔰ
    lenth++;//ᶲଧᤒᳩଶ+1
    
  }
  public void delete(int index) throws Exception {
    if(index<0||index>lenth-1)
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    for(int i=index;i<lenth;i++)//indexۖ
    {
      date[i]=date[i+1];
    }
    lenth--;//ᳩଶ-1  
  }
  @Override
  public void set(int index, T t) throws Exception {
    if(index<0||index>lenth-1)
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    date[index]=t;
  }
  public String  toString() {
    String vaString="";
    for(int i=0;i<lenth;i++)
    {
      vaString+=date[i].toString()+" ";
    }
    return vaString;
    
  }
}

ᅩ)޾length᱾ᤒᳩଶ҅ᬯӻᜓᅩํහഝऒ޾೰ᰒऒ̶හഝऒ੪ฎਂන፥ਫጱහഝ҅ᘒ೰

ଃ१ᕮᅩ᱾ᤒVSӧଃ१ᕮᅩ᱾ᤒ  
ӧଃ१ᕮᅩ҅౯ᕳय़ਹᴏᬿӞ
ӥғ
ଃ१ᕮᅩғheadአ(ፘ୮ԭቔԆձଃ਍ኞ)
ӧଃ१ᕮᅩғheadਂํපහ꧊̶

ັತӤ҅ଃ१ᕮᅩᵱᥝग़ತӞ̶ེ
Ӥғᶋᒫ0ᒫ0ݒhead̶
class node<T>{
    T data;//ᜓᅩጱᕮຎ
    node next;//ӥӞӻᬳളጱᜓᅩ
    public node(){}
    public node(T data)
    {
        this.data=data;
    }
    public node(T data, node next) {
        this.data = data;
        this.next = next;
    } 
}

public class Linkedlist<T> implements ListInterface<T>{

  public int ElemIndex(T t) {
    node team=head.next;
    int index=0;
    while(team.next!=null)
    {
      if(team.data.equals(t))
      {
        return index;
      }
      index++;
      team=team.next;
    }
    return -1;//ک
  }
  @Override
  public T getElem(int index) throws Exception {
    node team=head.next;
    if(index<0||index>length-1)
    {
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    }
    for(int i=0;i<index;i++)
    {
      team=team.next;
    }
    return (T) team.data;
  }
  public void add(T t) throws Exception {
    add(length,t);
    
  }
  //֢
  public void add(int index, T value) throws Exception {
    if(index<0||index>length)
    {
      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");
    }
    node<T> team=head;//team ᗝnode
    for(int i=0;i<index;i++)
    {
         team=team.next;

    node<T>node =new node(value);//ෛୌӞӻnode
    node.next=team.next;//ݻindexᗝጱӥӞӻ೰ᰒ
    team.next=node;//౮indexᗝ  
    length++;
  }

      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");

    }

    // TODO Auto-generated method stub

      throw new Exception("හ꧊᩼ኴ");

  }
  public String toString() {
    String va="";
    node team=head.next;
    while(team!=null)
    {
      va+=team.data+" ";
      team=team.next;
    }
    return va;
  }

public class doubleList<T> {
    class node<T> {
        T data;
        node<T> pre;
        node<T> next;
        public node() {
        }
        public node(T data) {
            this.data = data;
        }
    }
    private node<T> head;// १ᜓᅩ
    private node<T> tail;// ੲᜓᅩ
    private int length;
    public doubleList() {
        head = null;
        tail = head;
        length = 0;
    }
    boolean isEmpty() {
        return length == 0 ? true : false;
    }
    void addFirst(T data) {
        node<T> teamNode = new node(data);
        if (isEmpty()) {
            head = teamNode;
            tail = teamNode;
        } else {
            teamNode.next = head;
            head = teamNode;
        }
        length++;
    }
    void add(T data)// ف
{
        node<T> teamNode = new node(data);
        if (isEmpty()) {
            head = teamNode;
            tail = teamNode;
        } else {
            tail.next = teamNode;
            teamNode.pre=tail;
            tail = teamNode;
        }
        length++;
    }
    int length()
    {
        return length;
    }
    T getElum(int index)//ᕹӞ՗१ತ
    {
        node<T> team=head;
        for(int i=0;i<index;i++)//ӧଃ१ᜓᅩ  ེහ-1
        {
            team=team.next;
        }
        return team.data;
    }
    void add(int index, T data)// ف
{
        if (index == 0) {
            addFirst(data);
        } else if (index == length) {
            add(data);
        } else {// ᯿१౭
            node teampre = head;// ḝ
            // ෫१ᜓᅩ҅index-1ḝᜓᅩ
            for (int i = 0; i < index -1; i++)
            {
                teampre = teampre.next;
            }
            node<T> team = new node(data);// a c فB ತ಑a

=== PDF PAGE 20 ===
ၥᦶғ
            team.next = teampre.next;// B.next=c
            teampre.next.pre = team;// c.pre=B
            team.pre = teampre;// ᘶa B
            teampre.next = team;
            length++;
        }
    }
    void deleteFirst()// ᴻ
    {
        if (length == 1)// ᔰ
        {
            head = null;
            tail = head;
            length--;
        } else {
            head = head.next;
            length--;
        }
    }
    void deleteLast() {
        if(length==1)
        {
            head=null;
            tail=head;
            length--;
        }
        else {
            tail.pre.next=null;
            tail=tail.pre;
            length--;
        }
    }
    void delete(int index)
    {
        if(index==0)deleteFirst();
        else if (index==length-1) {
            deleteLast();
        }
        else {//ᴻ ԅԧቘᥴᕹӞ՗१ತᮎӻᜓᅩ
            node<T>team=head;
            for(int i=0;i<index-1;i++)
            {
                team=team.next;
            }
            //team ᜓᅩ  a  c   فB  aԅteam
            team.next.next.pre=team;//c౮a
            team.next=team.next.next;//a౮c
            length--;
        }
    }
    void set(int index,T data)
    {
        node<T>team=head;
        for(int i=0;i<index-1;i++)
        {
            team=team.next;
        }
        team.data=data;
    }
    @Override
    public String toString() {
        node<T> team = head;
        String vaString = "";
        while (team != null) {
            vaString += team.data + " ";
            team = team.next;
        }
        return vaString;
    }
}

package ᴚ຾;
public class seqStack<T> {
  
  private T data[];
  private int top;
  public seqStack() {
    data=(T[]) new Object[10];
    top=-1;
  }
  public seqStack(int maxsize)
  {
    data=(T[]) new Object[maxsize];
    top=-1;
  }
  boolean isEmpty()
  {
    return top==-1;
  }
  int length()
  {
    return top+1;
  }
  
  boolean push(T value) throws Exception//຾
  {
    if(top+1>data.length-1)
    {
      throw new Exception("຾૪ჿ");
    }
    else {
      data[++top]=value;
      return true;
    }
  }
  T peek() throws Exception//ᔰӧᑏᴻ
  {
    if(!isEmpty())
    {
      return data[top];
    }
    else {
      throw new Exception("຾ԅᑮ");
    }
  }
  T pop() throws Exception
  {
    if(isEmpty())
    {
      throw new Exception("຾ԅᑮ");
    }
    else {
       return data[top--];
    }
  }
  public String toString()
  {

    public T pop() throws Exception {//຾
      if(length==0) {throw new Exception("᱾ᤒԅᑮ");}
      else {//ᴻ
        T value=(T) head.next.data;
        head.next=head.next.next;//va.next
        length--;
        return value;
        }
    }
    public String toString(){
      if(length==0) {return "";}
      else {
        String va="";
        node team=head.next;
        while(team!=null)
        {
          va+=team.data+" ";
          team=team.next;
        }
        return va;
     }    
    }
}

public boolean isValid(String s) {
  char a[]=new char[s.length()];
  int index=-1;
   for(int i=0;i<s.length();i++)
   {  
     char te=s.charAt(i);
     if(te==']')
     {
       if(index>=0&&a[index]=='[')
         index--;
       else {
        return false;
      }
     }
     else if(te=='}')
     {
       if(index>=0&&a[index]=='{')
         index--;
       else {
        return false;
      }
     }
     else if(te==')')
     {
       if(index>=0&&a[index]=='(')
         index--;
       else {
        return false;
       }
     }
     else
       a[++index]=te;
   }
   return index==-1; 
 }

public  int longestValidParentheses(String s) {
  char str[]=s.toCharArray();//ਁᒧහᕟ
  int max=0;
  for(int i=0;i<str.length-1;i++)
  {
    int index=-1;
    if(max>=str.length-i)
      break;
    for(int j=i;j<str.length;j++)
    {
      if(str[j]=='(')
        index++;
      else {
        if(index<0)
        {
          i=j;
          break;
        }
        else {
          index--;
        }
      }
      if(index==-1&&(j-i+1>max))
      {
        max=j-i+1;
      }
    }
  } 
  return max;
}

 public static int longestValidParentheses(String s) {
    int max=0;  
    int value[]=new int[s.length()+1];
    int index=0;
    for(int i=0;i<s.length();i++)
    {
      if(s.charAt(i)=='(')
      {
        index++;
        value[index]=0;
      }
      else {//")"
        if(index==0)
        {
          value[0]=0;
        }
        else {
            value[index-1]+=value[index--]+2;//ےݖ
            if(value[index]>max)//ๅෛ
              max=value[index];
        }
      }
    }
    return max;
 }
public int longestValidParentheses(String s) {
  int maxans = 0;
  Stack<Integer> stack = new Stack<>();
  stack.push(-1);
  for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
    if (s.charAt(i) == '(') {//(ጱ 
      stack.push(i);
    } else {
      stack.pop();
      if (stack.empty()) {
        stack.push(i);
      } else {//i-stack.peek੪ฎiሿጱ௛ӻහ peekᯈጱӻහ
        maxans = Math.max(maxans, i - stack.peek());
      }
    }
  }
  return maxans;
}

public class MyCircularQueue {
    private int data[];// ࢏
    private int front;// १
    private int rear;// ੲ
    private int maxsize;// ๋य़ᳩଶ
    public MyCircularQueue(int k) {
        data = new int[k+1];
        front = 0;
        rear = 0;
        maxsize = k+1;
    }
    public boolean enQueue(int value)  {
        if (isFull())
            return  false;
        else {
            data[rear] = value;
            rear=(rear + 1) % maxsize;
        }
        return  true;
    }
    public boolean deQueue() {
        if (isEmpty())
            return false;
        else {
            front=(front+1)%maxsize;
        }
        return  true;
    }
    public int Front() {
        if(isEmpty())
            return -1;
        return data[front];
    }
    public int Rear() {
        if(isEmpty())
            return -1;
        return data[(rear-1+maxsize)%maxsize];
    }
    public boolean isEmpty() {
        return rear == front;
    }
    public boolean isFull() {
        return (rear + 1) % maxsize == front;
    }
}

}

public boolean isContains(int x)//ࣁ
{
  node current=root;
  if(root==null) {return false;}
  while(current.value!=x&&current!=null) 
  {
    if(x<current.value) {current=current.left;}
    if(x>current.value) {current=current.right;}
    if(current==null) {return false;}//ࢧ
  }
  //ԅᑮտ੕ᛘcurrent.valueಸᲙ
   if(current.value==x) {return true;}    
  return false;   
}
public node insert(int x)// فtฎrootጱ୚አ
{
  node current = root;
  if (root == null) {
    root = new node(x);
    return root;
  }
  while (current != null) {
    if (x < current.value) {
      if (current.left == null) {
        return current.left = new node(x);}
      else current = current.left;}
      else if (x > current.value) {
      if (current.right == null) {
        return current.right = new node(x);}
      else current = current.right;
    }
  }
  return current;//ک
}

public node remove(int x, node t)// ᴻᜓᅩ
{
  if (t == null) {
    return null;
  }
  if (x < t.value) {
    t.left = remove(x, t.left);
  } else if (x > t.value) {
    t.right = remove(x, t.right);
  } else if (t.left != null && t.right != null)// ӧᑮ
  {
    t.value = findmin(t.right).value;// ๋ੜ꧊๊դ
    t.right = remove(t.value, t.right);
  } else // ᮷ᑮ
  {
    if (t.left == null && t.right == null) {
      t = null;
    } else if (t.right != null) {
      t = t.right;
    } else if (t.left != null) {
      t = t.left;
    }
    return t;
  }
  return t;
}

public int[] levelOrder(TreeNode root) {
        int arr[]=new int[10000];
        int index=0;
        Queue<TreeNode>queue=new ArrayDeque<>();
        if(root!=null)
            queue.add(root);
        while (!queue.isEmpty()){
            TreeNode node=queue.poll();
            arr[index++]= node.val;
            if(node.left!=null)
                queue.add(node.left);
            if(node.right!=null)
                queue.add(node.right);
            
        }
        return Arrays.copyOf(arr,index);
    }

public List<List<Integer>> levelOrder(TreeNode root) {
  List<List<Integer>>list=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();
  if(root==null)return list;
  Queue<TreeNode>q1=new ArrayDeque<TreeNode>();
  q1.add(root);
  while (!q1.isEmpty()) {
    int size=q1.size();
    List<Integer>value=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    for(int i=0;i<size;i++)
    {
      TreeNode pNode=q1.poll();
      if(pNode.left!=null)
        q1.add(pNode.left);
      if(pNode.right!=null)
        q1.add(pNode.right);
      value.add(pNode.val);
    }
    list.add(value);
  }
  return list;
}

    for(int i=0;i<len;i++){
      TreeNode node=queue.poll();

        va.add(node.val);

class Solution {
    List<Integer>value=new ArrayList();
    public List<Integer> preorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
        qianxu(root);
        return value;
    }
    private void qianxu(TreeNode node) {
        if(node==null)
            return;
        value.add(node.val);
        qianxu(node.left);
        qianxu(node.right);
    }
}
class Solution {
  public List<Integer> preorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
    List<Integer>value=new ArrayList();
    Stack<TreeNode> q1 = new Stack(); 
    while(!q1.isEmpty()||root!=null)
    {
      while (root!=null) {
        value.add(root.val);
        q1.push(root);        
        root=root.left;
      }
            root=q1.pop();//ڊ
            root=root.right;//ᜓᅩ
      
    }
        return value;
    }
}

class Solution {
   public List<Integer> inorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
    List<Integer>value=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    zhongxu(root,value);
    return value;
  }
   private void zhongxu(TreeNode root, List<Integer> value) {
     if(root==null)
       return;
     zhongxu(root.left, value);
     value.add(root.val);
     zhongxu(root.right, value);
    
  }
}
class Solution {
   public List<Integer> inorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
  List<Integer>value=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    Stack<TreeNode> q1 = new Stack(); 
    while(!q1.isEmpty()||root!=null)
    {
        while (root!=null) {
            q1.push(root);        
            root=root.left;
        }
        root=q1.pop();//ڊ
        value.add(root.val);
        root=root.right;//ᜓᅩ
        
    }
    return value;
  }
}

class Solution {
    public List<Integer> postorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
        List<Integer> value=new ArrayList();
        Stack<TreeNode> q1 = new Stack(); 
        Map<TreeNode,Integer >map=new HashMap<>();
        while(!q1.isEmpty()||root!=null)
        {
            if (root!=null) {
                q1.push(root);
                map.put(root, 1); //t.valueሿጱེහ
                root=root.left;
            }
            else {
                root=q1.peek();
                if(map.get(root)==2) {//ڊ
                    q1.pop();
                    value.add(root.val);
                    root=null;//ᵱᥝஃӤᩳ
                }
                else {
                    map.put(root, 2);
                    root=root.right;
                }     
            }
          }
          return value;
    }
}
class Solution {
    public List<Integer> postorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
        TreeNode temp=root;//ຍԈጱԁ෸ᜓᅩ
        List<Integer>value=new ArrayList<>();
        TreeNode pre=null;//ᗝᜓᅩ
        Stack<TreeNode>stack=new Stack<>();
        while (!stack.isEmpty()||temp!=null){
        
            while(temp!=null){
                stack.push(temp);
                temp=temp.left;
            }
            temp=stack.pop();
            if(temp.right==pre||temp.right==null)//ڊ
{
                value.add(temp.val);
                pre=temp;
                temp=null;//ڊ
}else{
                stack.push(temp);
                temp=temp.right;
            }
            
        }

class Solution {
    public List<Integer> postorderTraversal(TreeNode root) {
      List<Integer>value=new ArrayList();
      Stack<TreeNode> q1 = new Stack(); 
      while(!q1.isEmpty()||root!=null)
      {
        while (root!=null) {
          value.add(root.val);
          q1.push(root);        
          root=root.right;
        }
        root=q1.pop();//ڊ
        root=root.left;//ᜓᅩ
      }
      Collections.reverse(value);//ਖ਼ᕮຎᘉ᫨
      return value;
     
    }
}

 * Definition for a binary tree node.
 * public class TreeNode {
 *     int val;
 *     TreeNode left;
 *     TreeNode right;
 *     TreeNode(int x) { val = x; }
 * }

class Solution {
   public TreeNode buildTree(int[] preorder, int[] inorder) {
    if(preorder.length==0)
      return null;
    TreeNode root=new TreeNode(preorder[0]);
    Map<Integer, Integer>map=new HashMap<Integer, Integer>();
    for(int i=0;i<inorder.length;i++)
    {
      map.put(inorder[i], i);
    }
    return buildTree(preorder,0,preorder.length-1, map,0,inorder.length-1);
    }
  private TreeNode buildTree(int[] preorder, int preStart, int preEnd, Map<Integer, 
Integer> map, int inStart, int inEnd) {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    if(preEnd<preStart||inEnd<inStart)
      return null;
    TreeNode node=new TreeNode(preorder[preStart]);
    int i=map.get(preorder[preStart]);//ᜓᅩጱ꧊
    
    int leftlen=i-inStart;//ૢᶎጱᳩଶ
    node.left=buildTree(preorder, preStart+1, preStart+1+leftlen, map, inStart, i-1);
    node.right=buildTree(preorder, preStart+leftlen+1,preEnd, map, i+1, inEnd);
    return node;    
  }
}

/**
 * Definition for a binary tree node.
 * public class TreeNode {
 *     int val;
 *     TreeNode left;
 *     TreeNode right;
 *     TreeNode(int x) { val = x; }
 * }
 */
class Solution {
   public TreeNode buildTree(int[] inorder,int[] postorder) {
    if(postorder.length==0)
      return null;
    Map<Integer, Integer>map=new HashMap<Integer, Integer>();
    for(int i=0;i<inorder.length;i++)
    {
      map.put(inorder[i], i);
    }
    return buildTree(postorder,0,postorder.length-1, map,0,inorder.length-1);
    }
  private TreeNode buildTree(int[] postorder, int postStart, int postEnd, Map<Integer, 
Integer> map, int inStart, int inEnd) {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    if(postEnd<postStart||inEnd<inStart)
      return null;
    TreeNode node=new TreeNode(postorder[postEnd]);
    int i=map.get(postorder[postEnd])Ҕ
    int leftlen=i-inStart;
    node.left=buildTree(postorder, postStart,postStart+leftlen-1, map, inStart, i-1);
    node.right=buildTree(postorder, postStart+leftlen,postEnd-1, map, i+1, inEnd);       
   
    return node;    
  }
}

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Queue;
public class AVLTree {
    class node
    {
        int value;
        node left;
        node right;
        int height;
        public node() {
        }
        public node(int value)
        {
            this.value=value;
            this.height=0;
        }
        public node(int value,node left,node right)
        {
            this.value=value;
            this.left=left;this.right=right;
            this.height=0;
        }
    }
    node root;// ໑
    public AVLTree() {
        this.root = null;
    }
    public boolean isContains(int x)// ࣁ
{

=== PDF PAGE 58 ===
        node current = root;
        if (root == null) {
            return false;
        }
        while (current.value != x && current != null) {
            if (x < current.value) {
                current = current.left;
            }
            if (x > current.value) {
                current = current.right;
            }
            if (current == null) {
                return false;
            } // ࢧ
}
        // ԅᑮտ੕ᛘcurrent.valueಸᲙ
        if (current.value == x) {
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }
    public int getHeight(node t)
    {
        if(t==null) {return -1;}//
        return t.height;
        //return 1+Math.max(getHeight(t.left), getHeight(t.right));֗
}
    public void cengxu(node t) {//ܲ
        Queue<node> q1 = new ArrayDeque<node>();
        if (t == null)
            return;
        if (t != null) {
            q1.add(t);
        }
        while (!q1.isEmpty()) {
            node t1 = q1.poll();
            if (t1.left != null)
                q1.add(t1.left);
            if (t1.right != null)
                q1.add(t1.right);
            System.out.print(t1.value + " ");
        }
        System.out.println();
    }
    public void zhongxu(node t)//ܲ ғૢৼ໅---> ໑ᕮᅩ ---> ৼ໅
    {//᮷ᤈ
        if(t!=null)
        {
            zhongxu(t.left);
            System.out.print(t.value+" ");//ᜓᅩ
            zhongxu(t.right);
            //System.out.print(t.value+" ");//ᜓᅩ
        }
    }
    public void qianxu(node t)//ଧ᭓୭ ғ໑ᕮᅩ ---> ૢৼ໅ ---> ৼ໅
    {

    }
    public void insert(int value) {
        root=insert(value, root);
    }

    private node banlance(node t) {
        // TODO Auto-generated method stub
        //if(t==null)return null;
        int lefthigh=getHeight(t.left);
        int righthigh=getHeight(t.right);
        if(Math.abs(lefthigh-righthigh)<=1)//ӧᵱᥝଘᤍᄆ
        { return t;}
        else if(lefthigh<righthigh)//य़
        {
            if(getHeight(t.right.left)<getHeight(t.right.right))//RRᵱᥝૢ෤
            {
                return  getRRbanlance(t);
            }
            else {
                return getRLbanlance(t);
            }
        }
        else {
            if(getHeight(t.left.left)>getHeight(t.left.right))//ll ૢૢ
            {
                return getLLbanlance(t);
            }
            else {
                return getLRbanlance(t);
            }
        }
    }
    /*
     *        oldroot(ৼԅ2,ӧଘᤍ)    ==>   newroot
     *       /    \                              /    \
     *      B     newroot(ৼԅ1)        oldroot   D
     *             /    \                      / \      \
     *            C      D                    B   C      E
     *                    \
     *                     E
     */
    private node getRRbanlance(node oldroot) {//Ⴎ҅ᵱᥝૢ෤
        // TODO Auto-generated method stub
        node newroot=oldroot.right;
        oldroot.right=newroot.left;
        newroot.left=oldroot;
        oldroot.height=Math.max(getHeight(oldroot.left),getHeight(oldroot.right))+1;
        newroot.height=Math.max(getHeight(newroot.left),getHeight(newroot.right))+1;//๶
ጱrootጱṛଶᵱᥝ՗ෛᦇᓒ
        return newroot;
    }

    private node getLLbanlance(node oldroot) {//LL෤᫨
        // TODO Auto-generated method stub
        node newroot=oldroot.left;
        oldroot.left=newroot.right;
        newroot.right=oldroot;
        oldroot.height=Math.max(getHeight(oldroot.left),getHeight(oldroot.right))+1;
        newroot.height=Math.max(getHeight(newroot.left),getHeight(newroot.right))+1;//๶
ጱrootጱṛଶᵱᥝ՗ෛᰂᯢ  
        return newroot;
    }
    private node getLRbanlance(node oldroot) {
        oldroot.left =getRRbanlance(oldroot.left);
        oldroot.height=Math.max(getHeight(oldroot.left), getHeight(oldroot.right))+1;
        return getLLbanlance(oldroot);
    }

     *           newroot   D         A      E    F   D

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.PriorityQueue;
import java.util.Queue;
public class HuffmanTree {  
  public static class node
  {
    int value;
    node left;
    node right;
    int deep;//ᦕ୯Ⴎଶ
    public node(int value) {
      this.value=value;
      this.deep=0;
    }
    public node(node n1, node n2, int value) {
      this.left=n1;
      this.right=n2;
      this.value=value;
    }
  }
  private node root;//ኞ౮ጱ໑ᜓᅩ
  List<node>nodes;
  public HuffmanTree() {
    this.nodes=null;
  }
  public HuffmanTree(List<node>nodes)
  {
    this.nodes=nodes;
  }
  public void createTree() {
     Queue<node>q1=new PriorityQueue<node>(new Comparator<node>() {
      public int compare(node o1, node o2) {
        return o1.value-o2.value;
      }});
     q1.addAll(nodes);
     while(!q1.isEmpty()){
       node n1=q1.poll();
       node n2=q1.poll();
      node parent=new node(n1,n2,n1.value+n2.value);
      if(q1.isEmpty()){
        root=parent;return;
      }
      q1.add(parent);
     }
  }
  public int getweight() {
    Queue<node>q1=new ArrayDeque<node>();
    q1.add(root);
    int weight=0;
    while (!q1.isEmpty()) {
      node va=q1.poll();
      if(va.left!=null){
        va.left.deep=va.deep+1;va.right.deep=va.deep+1;
        q1.add(va.left);q1.add(va.right);
      }
      else {
        weight+=va.deep*va.value;
      }
    }
    return weight;
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    List<node>list=new ArrayList<node>();
    list.add(new node(2));
    list.add(new node(3));
    list.add(new node(6));
    list.add(new node(8));list.add(new node(9));
    HuffmanTree tree=new HuffmanTree();
    tree.nodes=list;
    tree.createTree();
    System.out.println(tree.getweight());
  }
}

import java.util.Arrays;
public class priQueue {
    private  int size;//ጱय़ੜ
    private  int capacity;//හᕟጱ਻ᰁ
    private  int value[];//ਂጱ꧊
    public priQueue() {
        this.capacity = 10;
        this.value = new int[capacity];
        this.size=0;
    }
    public priQueue(int capacity) {
        this.capacity = capacity;
        this.value = new int[capacity];
        this.size=0;
    }
    /**
     * ᔰ
     * @param number
     */
    public void add(int number) {
        if(size==capacity)//ಘ਻
        {
            capacity*=1.5;
            value= Arrays.copyOf(value,capacity);
        }
        value[size++]=number;//๛ੲ
        int index=size-1;
        while (index>=1) {//ᬰᤈԻഘ
            if(value[index]<value[index/2]) {
                swap(index,index/2,value);
                index=index/2;
            }
            else//ྊ
                break;
        }
    }
    public int peek() {
        return  value[0];
    }
    /**

=== PDF PAGE 70 ===
ӻᔄၥᦶӞӥ፡፡ғ
     * ᴚ१
     * @return
     */
    public int pop() {
        int val=value[0];//හഝ᷐
        value[0]=value[--size];//ञ१
        int index=0,leftChild=0,rightChild=0;
        while (true)
        {
            leftChild=index*2+1;
            rightChild=index*2+2;
            if(leftChild>=size)//ٖ
                break;
            else if(rightChild<size&&value[rightChild]<value[index]&&value[rightChild]
<value[leftChild])
            {//਎ৼๅੜ
                swap(index,rightChild,value);
                index=rightChild;
            }
            else if(value[leftChild]<value[index])
            {//ૢ਎ৼๅੜ
                swap(index,leftChild,value);
                index=leftChild;
            }
            else //ӧᵱᥝ ਙᛔ૩๋ੜ
                break;
        }
        return  val;
    }
    //ᔰ
    public  void swap(int i,int j,int arr[]) {
        int team=arr[i];
        arr[i]=arr[j];
        arr[j]=team;
    }
    public int size() {
        return  size;
    }
}

TrieNode root;
/** ۸/* 
public Trie() {
    root=new TrieNode();
}
/** Inserts a word into the trie. */
public void insert(String word) {
    TrieNode node=root;//ԁ෸ᜓᅩአ๶ຍԈ
    for(int i=0;i<word.length();i++)//ຍԈਁᒧԀ
    {
        int index=word.charAt(i)-'a';//ک26ᗝ
        if(node.son[index]==null)//ୌ
        {
            node.son[index]=new TrieNode();
        }
        node=node.son[index];
    }
    node.isEnd=true;//Ӟӻᜓᅩ
}

public boolean search(String word) {
    TrieNode node=root;
    for(int i=0;i<word.length();i++)
    {
        int index=word.charAt(i)-'a';
        if(node.son[index]==null)//ԅnullࢧfalse
        {
            return false;
        }
        node=node.son[index];
    }
    return node.isEnd==true;
}
public boolean startsWith(String prefix) {
    TrieNode node=root;
    for(int i=0;i<prefix.length();i++)
    {
        int index=prefix.charAt(i)-'a';
        if(node.son[index]==null)
        {
            return false;
        }
        node=node.son[index];
    }
  //ࢧtrue
    return  true;
}

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
public  class Trie{
    class TrieNode{
        Map<Character,TrieNode> sonMap;
        boolean idEnd;
        public TrieNode()
        {
            sonMap=new HashMap<>();
        }
    }
    TrieNode root;
    public Trie()
    {
        root=new TrieNode();
    }
   
    public void insert(String word) {
        TrieNode node=root;
        for(int i=0;i<word.length();i++)
        {
            char ch=word.charAt(i);
            if(!node.sonMap.containsKey(ch))//ف
{
                node.sonMap.put(ch,new TrieNode());
            }
            node=node.sonMap.get(ch);
        }
        node.idEnd=true;
    }
    
    public boolean search(String word) {
        TrieNode node=root;
        for(int i=0;i<word.length();i++)
        {
            char ch=word.charAt(i);
            if(!node.sonMap.containsKey(ch))
            {
                return false;
            }
            node=node.sonMap.get(ch);
        }
        return node.idEnd==true;//஠ᶳຽᦕԅtrueᦤกํᧆਁᒧԀ
    }
    public boolean startsWith(String prefix) {
        TrieNode node=root;
        for(int i=0;i<prefix.length();i++)
        {
            char ch=prefix.charAt(i);
            if(!node.sonMap.containsKey(ch))
            {
                return false;
            }
            node=node.sonMap.get(ch);
        }
        return true;//ݢܨ
}
}

import java.util.Scanner;
public class DisjointSet {
  static int tree[]=new int[100000];//ᦡํ500ӻ꧊
  public DisjointSet()  {set(this.tree);}
  public DisjointSet(int tree[]) 
  {
    this.tree=tree;
    set(this.tree);
  }
  //ಅํ᮷ฎ-1 ݻ1᧔กฎᛔ૩҅
  //ᒫԫ҅-1༏຋ํ-ҁ-1҂ӻ
  public void set(int a[])
  {
    int l=a.length;
    for(int i=0;i<l;i++)
    {
      a[i]=-1;
    }
  }
  public int search(int a)//१ᜓᅩጱහ꧊
  {
    if(tree[a]>0)//᧔กฎৼᜓᅩ
    {
      return tree[a]=search(tree[a]);//ᖽ
    }
    else
      return a;
  }
  public int value(int a)//ࢧa໅ጱय़ੜҁӻහ҂
  {
    if(tree[a]>0)
    {
      return value(tree[a]);
    }
    else
      return -tree[a];
  }
  public void union(int a,int b)//ᤒᐏ a҅bଚ

=== PDF PAGE 81 ===
ᕮ᧍  
ࣁک҅ᘒӧᤩ᯻
ᕑ̶
 զՕᕨአଚັᵞਫሿӞӻᩳᭁ਴ੜ჋౭ဳѺ
ӻᩳᭁ਴჋౭  
ࢩ  
ࢶ(ࢶ()࣎ࣈhttp://81.68.83.253:8081/maze.html)ғ
ᶱፓgithubғhttps://github.com/javasmall/mazegameғ
 
ᔺ஬҅ᕪଉ஬ԭcodingབԅჂካ̶
  {
    int a1=search(a);//a໑
    int b1=search(b);//b໑
    if(a1==b1) {System.out.println(a+"޾"+b+"Ӟ༒໅Ӥ");}
    else {
    if(tree[a1]<tree[b1])//᧣አvalueහ
    {
      tree[a1]+=tree[b1];//ے  ے
      tree[b1]=a1;       //b໅౮ԅaݻaҔ
    }
    else
    {
      tree[b1]+=tree[a1];//ے  ے
      tree[a1]=b1;       //b໅౮ԅaݻaҔ
    }
    }
  }
  public static void main(String[] args)
  {   
    DisjointSet d=new DisjointSet();
    d.union(1,2);
    d.union(3,4);
    d.union(5,6);
    d.union(1,6);
    
    d.union(22,24);
    d.union(3,26);
    d.union(36,24);
    System.out.println(d.search(6));  //१
    System.out.println(d.value(6));     //य़ੜ
    System.out.println(d.search(22)); //१
    System.out.println(d.value(22));     //य़ੜ
  }
}

class SkipNode<T>
{
    int key;
    T value;
    SkipNode right,down;//ጱ೰ᰒ
    public SkipNode (int key,T value) {
        this.key=key;
        this.value=value;
    }
}
public class SkipList <T> {
    
    SkipNode headNode;//ݗف
    int highLevel;//᪡ᤒᔱ୚੶හ
    Random random;// አԭಭാᏝ૰
    final int MAX_LEVEL = 32;//๋य़ጱ੶
    SkipList(){
        random=new Random();
        headNode=new SkipNode(Integer.MIN_VALUE,null);
        highLevel=0;
    }
    //՜ොဩ
}
public SkipNode search(int key) {
    SkipNode team=headNode;
    while (team!=null) {
        if(team.key==key)
        {
            return  team;

    return null;
}

public void add(SkipNode node)
{
    int key=node.key;
    SkipNode findNode=search(key);
    if(findNode!=null)//ᬯӻkeyጱᜓᅩ
    {
        findNode.value=node.value;
        return;
    }
    Stack<SkipNode>stack=new Stack<SkipNode>();//ᜓᅩ
    SkipNode team=headNode;//ጱᜓᅩ   ӻᜓᅩ̶
    while (team!=null) {//֢ 
        if(team.right==null)//ᚆӥᴳ
        {
            stack.add(team);//ӥጱᜓᅩᦕ୯Ӟӥ
            team=team.down;
        }
        else if(team.right.key>key)//੔ತ
        {
            stack.add(team);//ӥጱᜓᅩᦕ୯Ӟӥ
            team=team.down;
        }
        else //ݦݻ
{
            team=team.right;
        }
    }
    int level=1;//ے(ෙ)
    SkipNode downNode=null;//ḝᜓᅩ(ܨdownতԅnull)
    while (!stack.isEmpty()) {
        //فnode
        team=stack.pop();//ᜓᅩ
        SkipNode nodeTeam=new SkipNode(node.key, node.value);//ୌ
        nodeTeam.down=downNode;//ݻ
        downNode=nodeTeam;//አ
        if(team.right==null) {//ԅnull ๛ੲ
            team.right=nodeTeam;
        }
        //॒ቘ
        else {//ӷᘏԏᳵ
            nodeTeam.right=team.right;
            team.right=nodeTeam;
        }
        //Ӥ
        if(level>MAX_LEVEL)//ࠩ
            break;

import java.util.Random;
import java.util.Stack;
class SkipNode<T>
{
    int key;
    T value;
    SkipNode right,down;//ጱ೰ᰒ
    public SkipNode (int key,T value) {
        this.key=key;
        this.value=value;
    }
}
public class SkipList <T> {
    SkipNode headNode;//ݗف
    int highLevel;//੶හ
    Random random;// አԭಭാᏝ૰
    final int MAX_LEVEL = 32;//๋य़ጱ੶
    SkipList(){
        random=new Random();
        headNode=new SkipNode(Integer.MIN_VALUE,null);
        highLevel=0;
    }
    public SkipNode search(int key) {
        SkipNode team=headNode;
        while (team!=null) {
            if(team.key==key)
            {
                return  team;
            }
            else if(team.right==null)//ᚆӥᴳ
            {
                team=team.down;
            }
            else if(team.right.key>key)//੔ತ
            {
                team=team.down;
            }
            else //ݦݻ
{
                team=team.right;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
    public void delete(int key)//ᴻӧᵱᥝᘍᡤ੶හ
    {
        SkipNode team=headNode;
        while (team!=null) {
            if (team.right == null) {//ᚆӥᴳ
                team=team.down;

=== PDF PAGE 95 ===
            }
            else if(team.right.key==key)//ᴻᜓᅩ
            {
                team.right=team.right.right;//ᜓᅩ
                team=team.down;//ᴻ
            }
            else if(team.right.key>key)//ӥ
            {
                team=team.down;
            }
            else { //׆ݦࣁ
                team=team.right;
            }
        }
        // ᯿ෛᦇᓒ੶ṛ
        while (headNode.right == null) {
            headNode= headNode.down;
           highLevel--;
        }
    }
    public void add(SkipNode node)
    {
    
        int key=node.key;
        SkipNode findNode=search(key);
        if(findNode!=null)//ᬯӻkeyጱᜓᅩ
        {
            findNode.value=node.value;
            return;
        }
        Stack<SkipNode>stack=new Stack<SkipNode>();//ᜓᅩ
        SkipNode team=headNode;//ጱᜓᅩ   ӻᜓᅩ̶
        while (team!=null) {//֢
            if(team.right==null)//ᚆӥᴳ
            {
                stack.add(team);//ӥጱᜓᅩᦕ୯Ӟӥ
                team=team.down;
            }
            else if(team.right.key>key)//੔ತ
            {
                stack.add(team);//ӥጱᜓᅩᦕ୯Ӟӥ
                team=team.down;
            }
            else //ݦݻ
{
                team=team.right;
            }
        }
        int level=1;//ے(ෙ)
        SkipNode downNode=null;//ḝᜓᅩ(ܨdownতԅnull)
        while (!stack.isEmpty()) {
            //فnode
            team=stack.pop();//ᜓᅩ
            SkipNode nodeTeam=new SkipNode(node.key, node.value);//ୌ
            nodeTeam.down=downNode;//ݻ
            downNode=nodeTeam;//አ
            if(team.right==null) {//ԅnull ๛ੲ
                team.right=nodeTeam;
            }
            //॒ቘ
            else {//ӷᘏԏᳵ
                nodeTeam.right=team.right;
                team.right=nodeTeam;
            }
            //Ӥ
            if(level>MAX_LEVEL)//ࠩ
                break;
            double num=random.nextDouble();//[0-1]ᵋ๢හ
            if(num>0.5)//ᬩ࿈ӧঅᕮ๳
                break;
            level++;
            if(level>highLevel)//ٖࢱ ݒheadᜓᅩ
            {
                highLevel=level;
                //ୌӞӻෛጱᜓᅩ
                SkipNode highHeadNode=new SkipNode(Integer.MIN_VALUE, null);
                highHeadNode.down=headNode;
                headNode=highHeadNode;//ݒhead
                stack.add(headNode);//ڊhead
            }

ሿ᪡ᤒᬮฎꂁਠᗦጱ(ᛔ३Ӟӥ)̶
 
ଉᥠᓒဩ  
        }
    }
    public void printList() {
        SkipNode teamNode=headNode;
        int index=1;
        SkipNode last=teamNode;
        while (last.down!=null){
            last=last.down;
        }
        while (teamNode!=null) {
            SkipNode enumNode=teamNode.right;
            SkipNode enumLast=last.right;
            System.out.printf("%-8s","head->");
            while (enumLast!=null&&enumNode!=null) {
                if(enumLast.key==enumNode.key)
                {
                    System.out.printf("%-5s",enumLast.key+"->");
                    enumLast=enumLast.right;
                    enumNode=enumNode.right;
                }
                else{
                    enumLast=enumLast.right;
                    System.out.printf("%-5s","");
                }
            }
            teamNode=teamNode.down;
            index++;
            System.out.println();
        }
    }
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SkipList<Integer>list=new SkipList<Integer>();
        for(int i=1;i<20;i++)
        {
            list.add(new SkipNode(i,666));
        }
        list.printList();
        list.delete(4);
        list.delete(8);
        list.printList();
    }
}

{

  digui();

}
static void digui(int time)
{
  if(time==0) {}//time==0ӧಗᤈ
  else {
    System.out.println("bigsaiڹtime: "+time);
    digui(time-1);
    System.out.println("bigsaiݸtime: "+time); 
  } 
}

static int jiecheng(int n)
{
  if(n==0)//0ጱᴤԙԅ1
  {
    return 1;
  }
  else {
    return n*jiecheng(n-1);//return n*(n-1)*jiecheng(n-2)=-------
  }
}

static long F(int n)
{
  if(n==1||n==2) {return 1;}
  else {
    return F(n-1)+F(n-2);
  }
}

static long F(int n,long record[])
{
  if(n==1||n==2) {return 1;}
  if(record[n]>0)
    return record[n];
  else
    record[n]=F(n-1,record)+F(n-2,record);
  return record[n];
}
public static void main(String[] args) {
  int n=6;
  long[] record = new long[n+1];
  System.out.println(F(n,record));
}

/**
 * Definition for singly-linked list.
 * public class ListNode {
 *     int val;
 *     ListNode next;
 *     ListNode() {}
 *     ListNode(int val) { this.val = val; }
 *     ListNode(int val, ListNode next) { this.val = val; this.next = next; }
 * }
 */
class Solution {
    public ListNode reverseList(ListNode head) {
        if(head==null||head.next==null)
            return  head;
        ListNode node =reverseList(head.next);//ጱ᱾ᤒᜓᅩ
        head.next.next=head;//ᛔ૩
        head.next=null;//ᛔ૩nextݻnull 
        return node;
    }
}
public void qianxu(node t)// ଧ᭓୭ ғ໑ᕮᅩ ---> ૢৼ໅ ---> ৼ໅
{
  if (t != null) {
    System.out.print(t.value + " ");// ᜓᅩ
    qianxu(t.left);
    qianxu(t.right);
  }
}
public void zhongxu(node t)// ܲ ғૢৼ໅---> ໑ᕮᅩ ---> ৼ໅
{
  if (t != null) {
    zhongxu(t.left);
    System.out.print(t.value + " ");// ᜓᅩ
    zhongxu(t.right);
  }
}

public void houxu(node t)// ܲ ғૢৼ໅ ---> ৼ໅ ---> ໑ᕮᅩ
{
  if (t != null) {
    houxu(t.left);
    houxu(t.right);
    System.out.print(t.value + " "); // ᜓᅩ
  }
}
private static void mergesort(int[] array, int left, int right) {
  int mid=(left+right)/2;
  if(left<right)
  {
    mergesort(array, left, mid);
    mergesort(array, mid+1, right);
    merge(array, left,mid, right);
  }
}
private static void merge(int[] array, int l, int mid, int r) {
  int lindex=l;int rindex=mid+1;
  int team[]=new int[r-l+1];
  int teamindex=0;
  while (lindex<=mid&&rindex<=r) {//ଚ
    if(array[lindex]<=array[rindex])
    {
      team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
    }
    else {        
      team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
    }
  }
  while(lindex<=mid)//ݢܨے
{
    team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
  }
  while(rindex<=r)
  {
    team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
  } 
  for(int i=0;i<teamindex;i++)
  {
    array[l+i]=team[i];

    private static void dfs(int[] arr, int val[],boolean[] jud, int index,String s) {
        System.out.println(s+Arrays.toString(val));
        if (index == arr.length){ }//ྊ᭓୭๵կ
        else{
            for (int i = 0; i < arr.length; i++) {
                if (!jud[i]) {//ӧᚆአጱ
                    int team=val[index];
                    val[index] = arr[i];
                    jud[i] = true;// አ
                    dfs(arr, val, jud, index + 1,s+"  _  ");
                    jud[i] = false;// ܻ
                    val[index]=team;
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

import java.util.Arrays;
public class EightQueens {
    static  int allnum=0;
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        boolean hang[]=new boolean[8];//ᤈ
        boolean lie[]=new boolean[8];//ڜ
        boolean zuoxie[]=new boolean[15];//ૢෑ๴
        boolean youxie[]=new boolean[15];//ෑ๴
        int map[][]=new int[8][8];//ࢶࣈ
        dfs(0,hang,lie,zuoxie,youxie,map);//݄
}
    private static void dfs(int hindex, boolean[] hang, boolean[] lie, boolean[] zuoxie, 
boolean[] youxie, int[][] map) {
        if(hindex==8){
            allnum++;
            printmap(map);//ڊmap
        }
        else{
            //hindexԅᤈ  iڜ
            for(int i=0;i<8;i++)
            {
                if(!hang[hindex]&&!lie[i]&&!zuoxie[hindex+i]&&!youxie[hindex+(7-i)])
                {
                    hang[hindex]=true;//ᦶറ
                    lie[i]=true;
                    zuoxie[hindex+i]=true;
                    youxie[hindex+(7-i)]=true;
                    map[hindex][i]=1;
                    dfs(hindex+1,hang,lie,zuoxie,youxie,map);//dfs
                    hang[hindex]=false;//ܻ
                    lie[i]=false;
                    zuoxie[hindex+i]=false;
                    youxie[hindex+(7-i)]=false;
                    map[hindex][i]=0;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    //ࢶࣈڊ
    private static void printmap(int[][] map) {
        System.out.println("ᒫ"+allnum+"ԅ");
        for(int a[]:map)
        {
            System.out.println(Arrays.toString(a));
        }
    }
}

public int searchInsert(int[] nums, int target) {
  if(nums[0]>=target)return 0;//ຏ
  if(nums[nums.length-1]==target)return nums.length-1;//ຏ
  if(nums[nums.length-1]<target)return nums.length;
  int left=0,right=nums.length-1;
  while (left<right) {
    int mid=(left+right)/2;
    if(nums[mid]==target)
      return mid;
    else if (nums[mid]>target) {
      right=mid;
    }
    else {
      left=mid+1;
    }
  }
  return left;
}

public void quicksort(int [] a,int left,int right)
{
  int low=left;
  int high=right;
  //տԾኞහᕟ᩼ኴѺѺѺvery importantѺѺѺ
  if(low>high)//౼ྊ๵կ
    return;
  int k=a[low];//᷐क़ᑮᳵk᮷ྲਙय̶़
  while(low<high)//ੜԭa[low],य़ԭa[low]̶
  {
    while(low<high&&a[high]>=k)//ᒫӞӻੜԭkྊ
    {
      high--;
    }
    //ᒫӞӻྲਙੜጱԧ
    a[low]=a[high];//کlowᗝ
    while(low<high&&a[low]<=k)//ࣁlowᒫӞӻय़ԭk׆ݦکa[high]ᗝ
    {
      low++;
    }
    a[high]=a[low];     
  }
  a[low]=k;//လ࿢ԏ
  quicksort(a, left, low-1);
  quicksort(a, low+1, right);   
}

private static void mergesort(int[] array, int left, int right) {
  int mid=(left+right)/2;
  if(left<right)
  {
    mergesort(array, left, mid);
    mergesort(array, mid+1, right);
    merge(array, left,mid, right);
  }
}
private static void merge(int[] array, int l, int mid, int r) {
  int lindex=l;int rindex=mid+1;
  int team[]=new int[r-l+1];
  int teamindex=0;
  while (lindex<=mid&&rindex<=r) {//ଚ
    if(array[lindex]<=array[rindex])
    {
      team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
    }
    else {        
      team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
    }
  }
  while(lindex<=mid)//ݢܨے
{
    team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
  }
  while(rindex<=r)
  {
    team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
  } 
  for(int i=0;i<teamindex;i++)
  {
    array[l+i]=team[i];
  }
}

๋ᬪᅩ੒

public int maxSubArray(int[] nums) {
    int max=maxsub(nums,0,nums.length-1);
    return max;
}
int maxsub(int nums[],int left,int right)
{
    if(left==right)
        return  nums[left];
    int mid=(left+right)/2;
    int leftmax=maxsub(nums,left,mid);//๋य़
    int rightmax=maxsub(nums,mid+1,right);//๋य़
    int midleft=nums[mid];//Ӿᳵஃૢ
    int midright=nums[mid+1];//ݦ
    int team=0;
    for(int i=mid;i>=left;i--)
    {
        team+=nums[i];
        if(team>midleft)
            midleft=team;
    }
    team=0;
    for(int i=mid+1;i<=right;i++)
    {
        team+=nums[i];
        if(team>midright)
            midright=team;
    }
    int max=midleft+midright;//Ӿᳵጱ๋य़꧊
    if(max<leftmax)
        max=leftmax;
    if(max<rightmax)
        max=rightmax;
    return  max;
}

    private static double search(node[] no, int left,int right) {
        int mid=(right+left)/2;
        double minleng=0;
        if(left==right) {return Double.MAX_VALUE;}
        else if(left+1==right) {minleng= (no[left].x-no[right].x)*(no[left].x-
no[right].x)+(no[left].y-no[right].y)*(no[left].y-no[right].y);}
        else minleng= min(search(no,left,mid),search(no,mid,right));
        int ll=mid;int rr=mid+1;
        while(no[mid].y-no[ll].y<=Math.sqrt(minleng)/2&&ll-1>=left) {ll--;}
        while(no[rr].y-no[mid].y<=Math.sqrt(minleng)/2&&rr+1<=right) {rr++;}
        for(int i=ll;i<rr;i++)
        {
            for(int j=i+1;j<rr+1;j++)
            {
                double team=0;
                if(Math.abs((no[i].x-no[j].x)*(no[i].x-no[j].x))>minleng) {continue;}
                else
                { 
                    team=(no[i].x-no[j].x)*(no[i].x-no[j].x)+(no[i].y-no[j].y)*(no[i].y-
no[j].y);
                    if(team<minleng)minleng=team;
                }
            }
        }
        return minleng;
    
    }
    private static double min(double a, double b) {
        // TODO ኞ౮ጱොဩਂ໑
        return a<b?a:b;
    }
    static Comparator<node>com=new Comparator<node>() {
        @Override
        public int compare(node a1, node a2) {
            // TODO ኞ౮ጱොဩਂ໑
            return a1.y-a2.y>0?1:-1;
        }};
    static class node
    {
        double x;
        double y;
        public node(double x,double y)
        {
            this.x=x;
            this.y=y;
        }
    }
}

static long F(int n,long record[])
{
  if(n==1||n==2) {return 1;}
  if(record[n]>0)
    return record[n];
  else
    record[n]=F(n-1,record)+F(n-2,record);
  return record[n];
}

  int n=6;
  long[] record = new long[n+1];
  System.out.println(F(n,record));
}

            dp[i]=dp[i-1]+dp[i-2];
        }
        return dp[n];
    }

public int maxSubArray(int[] nums) {
    int dp[]=new int[nums.length];
    int max=nums[0];
    dp[0]=nums[0];
    for(int i=1;i<nums.length;i++)
    {
      dp[i]=Math.max(dp[i-1]+nums[i],nums[i]);
      if(dp[i]>max)
        max=dp[i];
    }
    return max;
}

class Solution {
    public int lengthOfLIS(int[] nums) {
        int dp[]=new int[nums.length];
        int maxLen=1;
        dp[0]=1;
        for(int i=1;i<nums.length;i++){
            int max=0;//ᶎ ๛ੲහਁྲᛔ૩ੜ ๋ᳩ᭓ीৼԀ
            for(int j=0;j<i;j++){//ຍԈ
                //හਁ ଚӬᳩଶय़ԭᦕ୯ጱ๋ᳩ
                if(nums[j]<nums[i]&&dp[j]>max){
                    max=dp[j];
                }
            }
            dp[i]=max+1;//ᶎ๋ᳩ Ӥᛔ૩
            if(maxLen<dp[i])
                maxLen=dp[i];
        }
        return maxLen;
    }
}
class Solution {
    public int lengthOfLIS(int[] nums) {
        int length[]=new int[nums.length];
        int len=1;
        length[0]=nums[0];
        for(int i=1;i<nums.length;i++){
            int left=0,right=len;
            while (left<right){
                int mid=left+(right-left)/2;
                if(length[mid]<nums[i]){
                    left=mid+1;
                }else {
                    right=mid;
                }
            }
            length[left]=nums[i];
            if(right==len)
                len++;        
        }
        return len;
    }

class Solution {
    public int numDistinct(String s, String t) {
      char s1[]=s.toCharArray();
      char t1[]=t.toCharArray();
      int dp[]=new int[t1.length+1];
      dp[0]=1;//ےݖ
      for(int i=0;i<s1.length;i++)
      {
        for(int j=t1.length-1;j>=0;j--)
        {
          if(t1[j]==s1[i])
          {
            dp[j+1]+=dp[j];
          }
        }
      }
      return dp[t1.length];
    }
}

  static boolean isVisit[];
  public static void main(String[] args) {

    int map[][]=new int[7][7];
    isVisit=new boolean[7];
    map[0][1]=map[1][0]=1;
    map[0][2]=map[2][0]=1;
    map[0][3]=map[3][0]=1;
    
    map[1][4]=map[4][1]=1;
    map[1][5]=map[5][1]=1;
    map[2][6]=map[6][2]=1;
    map[3][6]=map[6][3]=1;
  
    isVisit[0]=true;
    dfs(0,map);//՗0ܲ
  }
  private static void dfs(int index,int map[][]) {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    System.out.println("ᦢᳯ"+(index+1)+"  ");
    for(int i=0;i<map[index].length;i++)//ັತᘶ᭗ᜓᅩ
    {
      if(map[index][i]>0&&isVisit[i]==false)
      {
        isVisit[i]=true;
        dfs(i,map);
      }
    }
    System.out.println((index+1)+"ᦢᳯᕮ๳ ");
  }
}

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Queue;
public class bfs {
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    List<Integer> map[]=new ArrayList[7];
    boolean isVisit[]=new boolean[7];
    for(int i=0;i<map.length;i++)//۸
    {
      map[i]=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    }
    map[0].add(1);map[0].add(2);map[0].add(3);
    map[1].add(0);map[1].add(4);map[1].add(5);
    map[2].add(0);map[2].add(6);
    map[3].add(0);map[3].add(6);
    map[4].add(1);
    map[5].add(1);
    map[6].add(2);map[6].add(3);
    
    Queue<Integer>q1=new ArrayDeque<Integer>();
    q1.add(0);isVisit[0]=true;
    while (!q1.isEmpty()) {
      int va=q1.poll();
      System.out.println("ᦢᳯ"+(va+1));
      for(int i=0;i<map[va].size();i++)
      {
        int index=map[va].get(i);
        if(!isVisit[index])
        {
          q1.add(index);
          isVisit[index]=true;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Queue;
import java.util.Scanner;
import java.util.Set;

=== PDF PAGE 135 ===
 
public class test2 {  
  static class node{
     int x;
     int y;
    String path="";
    public node() {}
    public node(int x,int y,String team)
    {
      this.x=x;
      this.y=y;
      this.path=team;
    }
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    Scanner sc=new Scanner(System.in);
    Set<String>set=new HashSet<String>();//ਂ๋ᕣᕮຎ
    int n=Integer.parseInt(sc.nextLine());
    char map[][]=new char[n][n];
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
    {
      String string=sc.nextLine();
      map[i]=string.toCharArray();
    }
    Queue<node>q1=new ArrayDeque<node>();//ڜ
    Queue<node>q2=new ArrayDeque<node>();//ڜ
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
    {
      q1.clear();q2.clear();
      Set<String>set1=new HashSet<String>();//ਂzuoshang
      Set<String>set2=new HashSet<String>();//ӥ
      q1.add(new node(i,n-1-i,""+map[i][n-1-i]));
      q2.add(new node(i,n-1-i,""+map[i][n-1-i]));
      while(!q1.isEmpty()&&!q2.isEmpty())
      {
        node team=q1.poll();
        node team2=q2.poll();
        if(team.x==n-1&&team.y==n-1)//ਂ
        {
          //System.out.println(team2.path); 
          set1.add(team.path);
          set2.add(team2.path);
        }
        else {
          if(team.x<n-1)//ӥ
          {
            q1.add(new node(team.x+1, team.y, team.path+map[team.x+1][team.y]));
          }
          if(team.y<n-1)//ݦݻ
          {
            q1.add(new node(team.x, team.y+1, team.path+map[team.x][team.y+1]));
          }
          if(team2.x>0)//Ӥ
          {
            q2.add(new node(team2.x-1, team2.y, team2.path+map[team2.x-1][team2.y]));
          }
          if(team2.y>0)//ૢ
          {
            q2.add(new node(team2.x, team2.y-1, team2.path+map[team2.x][team2.y-1]));
          }
        }
        
      }
      for(String va:set1)
      {
        if(set2.contains(va))
        {
          set.add(va);
        }
      }
      
    }
    System.out.println(set.size());   
  }
}

import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Queue;
import java.util.Stack;
public class tuopu {
  static class node
  {
    int value;
    List<Integer> next;
    public node(int value) {
      this.value=value;
      next=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    }
    public void setnext(List<Integer>list) {
      this.next=list;
    }
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    node []nodes=new node[9];//ਂᜓᅩ
    int a[]=new int[9];//ଶ
    List<Integer>list[]=new ArrayList[10];//ݳ
    for(int i=1;i<9;i++)
    {
      nodes[i]=new node(i);
      list[i]=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    }
    initmap(nodes,list,a);
    
    //Ԇᥝၞᑕ
    //Queue<node>q1=new ArrayDeque<node>();
    Stack<node>s1=new Stack<node>();
    for(int i=1;i<9;i++)
    {
      //System.out.print(nodes[i].next.size()+" 55 ");
      //System.out.println(a[i]);
      if(a[i]==0) {s1.add(nodes[i]);}
      
    }
    while(!s1.isEmpty())
    {
      node n1=s1.pop();//ڊ
      System.out.print(n1.value+" ");
      List<Integer>next=n1.next;
      for(int i=0;i<next.size();i++)
      {
        a[next.get(i)]--;//Ӟ
        if(a[next.get(i)]==0)//ଶԅ0
        {
          s1.add(nodes[next.get(i)]);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  private static void initmap(node[] nodes, List<Integer>[] list, int[] a) {
    list[1].add(3);
    nodes[1].setnext(list[1]);
    a[3]++;
    list[2].add(4);list[2].add(6);
    nodes[2].setnext(list[2]);
    a[4]++;a[6]++;
    list[3].add(5);
    nodes[3].setnext(list[3]);
    a[5]++;
    list[4].add(5);list[4].add(6);
    nodes[4].setnext(list[4]);
    a[5]++;a[6]++;
    list[5].add(7);
    nodes[5].setnext(list[5]);
    a[7]++;
    list[6].add(8);
    nodes[6].setnext(list[6]);
    a[8]++;
    list[7].add(8);
    nodes[7].setnext(list[7]);
    a[8]++;
    
  }

რ๋Ꭸ᪠ஆDijkstra

class Solution {
    public boolean canFinish(int numCourses, int[][] prerequisites) {
        int indegree[]=new int[numCourses];
        List<Integer> next[]=new ArrayList[numCourses];
        
        for(int i=0;i<numCourses;i++){
            next[i]=new ArrayList();
        }
        for(int i=0;i<prerequisites.length;i++) {
            int preid=prerequisites[i][1];
            int courseid=prerequisites[i][0];
            indegree[courseid]++;//Ӟ
            next[preid].add(courseid);//nextݻ
}
        Queue<Integer>queue=new ArrayDeque<>();
        for(int i=0;i<numCourses;i++) {//ଶԅ0ጱᜓᅩ
            if(indegree[i]==0){
                queue.add(i);
            }
        }
        int nodeNum=0;//ᴻᜓᅩහᰁ ଶԅ0ᴻ ࢧtrue
        while (!queue.isEmpty())
        {
            nodeNum++;
            int nodeId=queue.poll();
            for(int i=0;i<next[nodeId].size();i++)
            {
                int nodeIndex=next[nodeId].get(i);
                indegree[nodeIndex]--;
                if(indegree[nodeIndex]==0) {
                    queue.add(nodeIndex);
                }
            }
        }
        if(nodeNum==numCourses)
            return true;
        return false;
    }
}

public class floyd {
  static int max = 66666;// ڦIntege.max ᩼ኴԅᨮ
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    int dist[][] = {
        { 0, 2, 3, 6, max, max }, 
        { 2, 0, max, max,4, 6 }, 
        { 3, max, 0, 2, max, max },
        { 6, max, 2, 0, 1, 3 }, 
        { max, 4, max, 1, 0, max }, 
        { max, 6, max, 3, max, 0 } };// ࢶࣈ
    6ӻ
    for (int k = 0; k < 6; k++)// ᒫkӻᜓᅩᬰᤈᦇᓒ
    {
      for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)// զᤩๅෛጱ
      {
        for (int j = 0; j < 6; j++)
        {
          dist[i][j] = Math.min(dist[i][j], dist[i][k] + dist[k][j]);
        }
      }
    }
    // ڊ
    for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      System.out.print("ᜓᅩ"+(i+1)+" ጱ๋Ꭸ᪠ஆ");
      for (int j = 0; j < 6; j++) {
        System.out.print(dist[i][j]+" ");
      }
      System.out.println();
    }
  }
}

class Solution {
    public int findTheCity(int n, int[][] edges, int distanceThreshold) {
        int dist[][]=new int[n][n];
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++){
              //य़(ӧᚆྲਙय़)҅ӧᥝInt๋य़ Კ
                dist[i][j]=1000000;
            }
            dist[i][i]=0;
        }
        for(int arr[]:edges){
            dist[arr[0]][arr[1]]=arr[2];
            dist[arr[1]][arr[0]]=arr[2];
        }
        for(int k=0;k<n;k++){
            for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
                for(int j=0;j<n;j++){
                    dist[i][j] = Math.min(dist[i][j], dist[i][k] + dist[k][j]);
                }
            }
        }
        int min=Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        int minIndex=0;
        int pathNum[]=new int[n];//᪗ᐶ
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                if(dist[i][j]<=distanceThreshold){
                    pathNum[i]++;
                }
            }
            if(pathNum[i]<=min) {
                min = pathNum[i];
                minIndex=i;
            }
        }
        return  minIndex;
    }
}

                dist[i][j]=1000000;
            }
            dist[i][i]=0;
        }
        for(int arr[]:edges){
            dist[arr[0]][arr[1]]=arr[2];
            dist[arr[1]][arr[0]]=arr[2];
        }

         for(int k=0;k<n;k++){
            for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
                for(int j=i+1;j<n;j++){//ധ᯿॔ጱᦇᓒ
                    dist[i][j] = Math.min(dist[i][j], dist[i][k] + dist[k][j]);
                    dist[j][i]=dist[i][j];
                }
            }
        }
        int min=Integer.MAX_VALUE;
        int minIndex=0;
        int pathNum[]=new int[n];//
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++) {
            for(int j=0;j<n;j++) {
                if(dist[i][j]<=distanceThreshold){
                    pathNum[i]++;
                }
            }
            if(pathNum[i]<=min) {
                min = pathNum[i];
                minIndex=i;
            }
        }
        return  minIndex;
    }

public class prim {
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    int minlength=0;//๋ੜኞ౮໅ጱ๋Ꭸ᪠ஆᳩଶ
    int max=66666;
    String cityname[]= {"Ղ","ྎ࿥","Ղ","Ӥၹ","๺૞","ଠ૞","ࣉ"};
    int city[][]= {
        { max, 8, 7, max, max, max, max }, //Ղᘶ᭗

        { 7, 6, max, 3,4, max,max }, //Ղ——Ղ̵ྎ࿥̵Ӥၹ̵๺૞

        { max, 8,max, max,max, max,2 }, //ଠ૞——ࣉ
        { max, max,max, max,10,2,max }//ࣉ——๺૞̵ଠ૞

      public int compare(side o1, side o2) {

        return o1.lenth-o2.lenth;

        istrue[2]=true;

      }
    }

    {
      side newside=q1.poll();//ڊ
      if(istrue[newside.point1]&&istrue[newside.point2])

        if(!istrue[newside.point1])
        {
          istrue[newside.point1]=true;
          minlength+=city[newside.point1][newside.point2];
          System.out.println(cityname[newside.point1]+" "+cityname[newside.point2]+" ᘶ
᭗");
          for(int i=0;i<7;i++)

package ᦞ;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.PriorityQueue;
import java.util.Queue;
import ᦞ.prim.side;
/*
 * ᘏғbigsai(ݩ)
 /*
public class kruskal {
  static int tree[]=new int[10];//bingັᵞ
  public static void init() {
    for(int i=0;i<10;i++)//ত
    {
      tree[i]=-1;
    }
  }
  public static int search(int a)//१ᜓᅩጱහ꧊
  {
    if(tree[a]>0)//᧔กฎৼᜓᅩ
    {
      return tree[a]=search(tree[a]);//ᖽ
    }
    else
      return a;
  }
  public static void union(int a,int b)//ᤒᐏ a҅bଚय़໅(ӧ᯿ᥝ)

=== PDF PAGE 155 ===
  {
    int a1=search(a);//a໑
    int b1=search(b);//b໑
    if(a1==b1) {//System.out.println(a+"޾b+"Ӟ༒໅Ӥ");
    }
    else {
    if(tree[a1]<tree[b1])//᧣አvalueහ
    {
      tree[a1]+=tree[b1];//ے  ے
      tree[b1]=a1;       //b໅౮ԅaݻaҔ
    }
    else
    {
      tree[b1]+=tree[a1];//ے  ے
      tree[a1]=b1;       //b໅౮ԅaݻaҔ
    }
    }
  }
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    init();
    int minlength=0;//๋ੜኞ౮໅ጱ๋Ꭸ᪠ஆᳩଶ
    int max=66666;
    String cityname[]= {"Ղ","ྎ࿥","Ղ","Ӥၹ","๺૞","ଠ૞","ࣉ"};
    boolean jud[][]=new boolean[7][7];//ᬟᵱᥝᴠྊ᯿॔ ྲই ba޾abᒵհጱ
    int city[][]= {
        { max, 8, 7, max, max, max, max }, 
        { 8, max,6, max,9, 8,max }, 
        { 7, 6, max, 3,4, max,max }, 
        { max, max,3, max,2, max,max }, 
        { max, 9,4, 2,max, max,10 }, 
        { max, 8,max, max,max, max,2 }, 
        { max, max,max, max,10,2,max }
    };// ࢶࣈ
    boolean istrue[]=new boolean[7];
    //Ղ
    Queue<side>q1=new PriorityQueue<side>(new Comparator<side>() {//ਂᬟ+
      public int compare(side o1, side o2) {
        // TODO Auto-generated method stub
        return o1.lenth-o2.lenth;
      }
    });
    for(int i=0;i<7;i++)
    {
      for(int j=0;j<7;j++)
      {
        if(!jud[i][j]&&city[i][j]!=max)//ڜ
        {
          jud[i][j]=true;jud[j][i]=true;
          q1.add(new side(city[i][j], i, j));
        }
      }
    }
    while(!q1.isEmpty())//ಗᤈᓒဩ
    {
      side newside=q1.poll();
      int p1=newside.point1;
      int p2=newside.point2;
      if(search(p1)!=search(p2))
      {
        union(p1, p2);
        System.out.println(cityname[p1]+" "+cityname[p2]+" ᘶ᭗");
        minlength+=newside.lenth;
      }
    }
    System.out.println(minlength);
    
  }
  static class side//ᬟ
  {
    int lenth;
    int point1;
    int point2;
    public side(int lenth,int p1,int p2) {
      this.lenth=lenth;
      this.point1=p1;
      this.point2=p2;
    }
  }
}

long c=10000007;
public  long divide(long a, long b) {
    if (b == 0)
      return 1;
    else if (b % 2 == 0) //٭
      return divide((a % c) * (a % c), b / 2) % c;
    else//٭
      return a % c * divide((a % c) * (a % c), (b - 1) / 2) % c;
  }

//࿢ a^b%1000000007
long c = 1000000007;
public  long divide(long a, long b) {
  a %= c;
  long res = 1;
  for (; b != 0; b /= 2) {
    if (b % 2 == 1)
      res = (res * a) % c;
    a = (a * a) % c;
  }
  return res;
}
f(n+1) = f(n)   + f(n-1)
f(n)   = f(n)
[f(n+1),f(n)]
=[f(n)+f(n-1),f(n)]
                 [1  1]
=[f(n),f(n-1)]  *      
                 [1  0]
                 
                 [1  1] [1   1]
=[f(n-1),f(n-2)]*      *
                 [1  0] [1   1]

public int Fibonacci(int n)
{
  n--;//Ꭵᴣԅӷᶱ
  int a[][]= {{1,1},{1,0}};//ᬰᤈள᭛଍ጱᎥᴣ
  int b[][]={{1,0},{0,1}};//Ꭵᴣ
  int time=0;
  while(n>0)
  {
    if(n%2==1)
    {
      b=matrixMultiplication(a, b);
    }
    a=matrixMultiplication(a, a);
    n/=2;
  }
  return b[0][0];
}
 public  int [][]matrixMultiplication(int a[][],int b[][]){//
   int x=a.length;//a[0].length=b.length ԅჿ᪃๵կ
   int y=b[0].length;//ӻ
   int c[][]=new int [x][y];
   for(int i=0;i<x;i++)
     for(int j=0;j<y;j++)
     {
       //ᔰ
       //c[i][j];
       for(int t=0;t<b.length;t++)
       {
         c[i][j]+=(a[i][t]%10000)*(b[t][j]%10000);
         c[i][j]%=10000;
       }
     }
   return c;
 }
 class Solution {
    public double myPow(double x, int n) {
        if(n==0)
            return 1;
        if(n==1)
            return x;
        if(n<0){
            return (1/x)*myPow((1/x),-(n+1));//ӧᥝ-n-1ڊ
}
        if(n%2==0)
            return myPow(x*x,n/2);
        else
            return x*myPow(x*x,(n-1)/2);
    }
}

public class Solution {
      public int Add(int num1,int num2) {
    /*
     *  5+3   5^3(0110)   5&3(0001) 
     *  0101    
     *  0011 
     */
    int a=num1^num2;
    int b=num1&num2;
    b=b<<1;
    if(b==0)return a;
    else {
      return Add(a, b);
    }        
   }
}

public int NumberOf1(int n) {
  int va=0;
  for(int i=0;i<32;i++)
  {
    if((n&(1<<i))!=0)
    {             
      va++;
    }
  }
  return va;        
}

public class Solution {
    public int NumberOf1(int n) {
     int count=0;
     while (n!=0) {
       n=n&(n-1);
       count++;
     }
     return count;
  }
}
class Solution {
    public int singleNumber(int[] nums) {
        int value=0;
        for(int i=0;i<nums.length;i++)
        {
            value^=nums[i];
        }
        return value;
    }
}

class Solution {
    public int singleNumber(int[] nums) {
        int value=0;
        for(int i=0;i<32;i++)
        {
            int sum=0;
            for(int num:nums)
            {
                if(((num>>i)&1)==1)
                {
                    sum++;
                }
            }
            if(sum%3==1)
                value+=(1<<i);
        }
        return value;
    }
}

private int getFirst1(int val) {
    int index=0;
    while (((val&1)==0&&index<32))
    {
        val>>=1;// val=val/2
        index++;
    }
    return index;
}

ཾ೉ጱ௏᪠੪ฎᐶ౯᫾ᬪጱ౯ᕳਙຽᦕ̶ཾ೉ᓀጱ෸ᳵ॔๥ଶԅO(n)ຽᦕӞ̶ེ
void getprime()
{
    prime=new long[100001];//ӻprime
    int index=0;//ຽᦕprimeӥຽ
    isprime=new boolean [1000001];//ᤩຽᦕᬦ
    for(int i=2;i<1000001;i++)
    {
      if(!isprime[i])
      {
        prime[index++]=i;
      }
      for(int j=i+i;j<1000000;j=j+i)//හ᮷over
      {
        isprime[j]=true;          
      }
    }
}
boolean isprime[];
int prime[];
void getprimeoula()// ཾ೉ᓀ
{
    prime = new int[100001];// ӻprime
    int index = 0;
    isprime = new boolean[1000001];
    for (int i = 2; i < 1000001; i++) {
      if (!isprime[i]) {
        prime[index++] = i;
      }
      for (int j = 0; j < index && i * prime[j] <= 100000; j++){//ຍԈ
        isprime[i * prime[j]] = true;// ຽᦕԙᑌ
        if (i % prime[j] == 0)
          break;
      }
    }
}

List<List<Integer>> list;
public List<List<Integer>> permuteUnique(int[] nums) {
    list=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();//๋ᕣጱᕮຎ
    List<Integer> team=new ArrayList<Integer>();//ᔰ
    boolean jud[]=new boolean[nums.length];//አ๶ຽᦕ
    dfs(jud, nums, team, 0);
    return list;
}
private  void dfs(boolean[] jud, int[] nums, List<Integer> team, int index) {
    int len = nums.length;
    if (index == len)// ྊ
    {
        list.add(new ArrayList<Integer>(team));
    } else
        for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) {
            if (jud[i]) //හਁᤩአᬦ አ
                continue;
            team.add(nums[i]);
            jud[i]=true;//አ
            dfs(jud, nums, team, index + 1);
            jud[i] = false;// ܻ
            team.remove(index);//ݳ
}
}

List<List<Integer>> list;
public List<List<Integer>> permuteUnique(int[] nums) {
  list=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();
  List<Integer> team=new ArrayList<Integer>();
  boolean jud[]=new boolean[nums.length];
  Arrays.sort(nums);
  dfs(jud, nums, team, 0);
  return list;
}
private  void dfs(boolean[] jud, int[] nums, List<Integer> team, int index) {
  // TODO Auto-generated method stub
  int len = nums.length;
  if (index == len)// ྊ
  {
    list.add(new ArrayList<Integer>(team));
  } else
    for (int i = 0; i < len; i++) {
        if (jud[i]||(i>0&&nums[i]==nums[i-1]&&!jud[i-1])) //හਁᤩአᬦ Ӟӻፘᒵጱᬮဌ
አ
          continue;
        team.add(nums[i]);
        jud[i]=true;
        dfs(jud, nums, team, index + 1);
        jud[i] = false;// ܻ
        team.remove(index);
    }
}

public List<List<Integer>> permuteUnique(int[] nums) {
     List<List<Integer>> list=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();
     arrange(nums, 0, nums.length-1, list);
     return list;
   }
private void arrange(int[] nums, int start, int end, List<List<Integer>> list) {
    if(start==end)
    {
      List<Integer>list2=new ArrayList<Integer>();
      for(int a:nums)
      {
        list2.add(a);
      }
      list.add(list2);
    }
    Set<Integer>set=new HashSet<Integer>();   
    for(int i=start;i<=end;i++)
    {
      if(set.contains(nums[i]))
        continue;
             set.add(nums[i]);       
      swap(nums,i,start);
      arrange(nums, start+1, end, list);
      swap(nums, i, start);
    } 
}
private void swap(int[] nums, int i, int j) {
  int team=nums[i];
  nums[i]=nums[j];
  nums[j]=team;
}

class Solution { 
  public List<List<Integer>> combine(int n, int k) {
    List<List<Integer>> valueList=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();//ᕮຎ
    int num[]=new int[n];//ؙ1-n
    boolean jud[]=new boolean[n];//አ
    for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
    {
      num[i]=i+1;
    }
  
    List<Integer>team=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    dfs(num,-1,k,valueList,jud,n);
    return valueList;
    }
  private void dfs(int[] num,int index, int count,List<List<Integer>> valueList,boolean 
jud[],int n) {
    if(count==0)//kᔰჿ
    {
      List<Integer>list=new ArrayList<Integer>();
      for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
      {
        if (jud[i]) {
          list.add(i+1);
        }
      }
      valueList.add(list);
    }
    else {
      for(int i=index+1;i<n;i++)//ࣁindexܲ ӥ
      {
        jud[i]=true;
        dfs(num, i, count-1, valueList,jud,n);
        jud[i]=false;//ܻ
      
      }
    } 
  }
}

class Solution {
   public List<List<Integer>> subsets(int[] nums) {
    List<List<Integer>> valueList=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();
    boolean jud[]=new boolean[nums.length];
    List<Integer>team=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    dfs(nums,-1,valueList,jud);
    return valueList;
    }
  private void dfs(int[] num,int index,List<List<Integer>> valueList,boolean jud[]) {
    {//ᕮຎӾ
      List<Integer>list=new ArrayList<Integer>();
      for(int i=0;i<num.length;i++)
      {
        if (jud[i]) {
          list.add(num[i]);
        }
      }
      valueList.add(list);
    }
    {
      for(int i=index+1;i<num.length;i++)
      {
        jud[i]=true;
        dfs(num, i, valueList,jud);
        jud[i]=false;
      
      }
    }
  }
}

class Solution {
  public List<List<Integer>> subsetsWithDup(int[] nums) {
    Arrays.sort(nums);
    boolean jud[]=new boolean[nums.length];
    List<List<Integer>> valueList=new ArrayList<List<Integer>>();
    dfs(nums,-1,valueList,jud);
    return valueList;
  }
  private void dfs(int[] nums, int index, List<List<Integer>> valueList, boolean[] jud)   
{
    // TODO Auto-generated method stub
    List<Integer>list=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    for(int i=0;i<nums.length;i++)
    {
      if (jud[i]) {
         list.add(nums[i]);
      }
    }
    valueList.add(list);
    for(int i=index+1;i<nums.length;i++)
    {//ᔰ ౲ ݶ  զᖀᖅᬰᤈ
      if((i==0)||(nums[i]!=nums[i-1])||(i>0&&jud[i-1]&&nums[i]==nums[i-1]))
      {
        jud[i]=true;
        dfs(nums, i, valueList,jud);
        jud[i]=false;
      }
    }
  }
}

public double findMedian() {
    if(list.size()%2==1)
        return list.get(list.size()/2);
    else {
        return (double) (list.get((list.size()-1)/2)+list.get(list.size()/2))/2;
    } 
}

    int arr[]=new int[50000];

    public void addNum(int num) {
        int low=0,high=count-1;
        while (low <= high) {
            int mid = (low + high) / 2;
            int midVal = arr[mid];
            if (midVal < num)
                low = mid + 1;
            else if (midVal > num)
                high = mid-1 ;
            else 
                low++;
        }
        
        for(int i=count-1;i>=low;i--){
            arr[i+1]=arr[i];

class MedianFinder {
    /** initialize your data structure here. */
    public MedianFinder() {
    }
    Queue<Integer>q1=new PriorityQueue<>();//ੜ໑ञ නय़ጱහഝ
    //य़໑ञ
    Queue<Integer>q2=new PriorityQueue<>(new Comparator<Integer>() {
        @Override
        public int compare(Integer o1, Integer o2) {
            return o2-o1;
        }
    });
    public void addNum(int num) {

public String addStrings(String num1, String num2) {
// ғbigsai ဳ
    int len1=num1.length()-1,len2=num2.length()-1;
    char ch1[]=num1.toCharArray();
    char ch2[]=num2.toCharArray();
    StringBuilder sb=new StringBuilder();
    int remainder =0;//හ
    while (len1>=0||len2>=0)
    {
        int n1=len1>=0?(ch1[len1--]-'0'):0;
        int n2=len2>=0?(ch2[len2--]-'0'):0;
        int num=n1+n2+remainder;//੒ଫහਁ
        remainder=num/10;//֖
        sb.append(num%10);// ᕮຎਁᒧԀӾ
    }
    if(remainder>0)//֖
{
        sb.append(remainder);
    }
    //ԅᕮຎ
    return sb.reverse().toString();
}

public static boolean compare(String num1,String num2)
{
    if(num1.length()<num2.length())
        return  false;
    else if(num1.length()>num2.length())
        return true;
    else
        return num1.compareTo(num2)>0;
}

//ᦡ num1>num2
public static String divideString(String num1,String num2)
{
    String value="0";//ᕮຎ
    while (compare(num1,num2))
    {
        StringBuilder sbTeam=new StringBuilder(num2);//ے0 ޾numဩ
        StringBuilder sbCount=new StringBuilder("1");//ེහ ᚆஉय़
        int subLen=num1.length()-num2.length();//ӻᵭ 
        for(int i=0;i<subLen;i++)
        {
            sbTeam.append('0');
            sbCount.append('0');
        }
        //ইຎ0 ग़ԧ Ӟӻ ֒12300" / "23" "12300"ྲ"23000"ੜ
        //ಅզᥝ "2300" ੒ଫྲ"23"ಘय़ "100"Ӟེ"2300" ے100"
        if(!compare(num1,sbTeam.toString()))
        {
            sbTeam=sbTeam.deleteCharAt(sbTeam.length()-1);
            sbCount=sbCount.deleteCharAt(sbCount.length()-1);
        }
        // ײ
        while (compare(num1,sbTeam.toString()))
        {
            num1=subtractString(num1,sbTeam.toString());
            value=addStrings(sbCount.toString(),value);
        }
    }
    return  value;
}

/**
 * Definition for singly-linked list.
 * public class ListNode {
 *     int val;
 *     ListNode next;
 *     ListNode(int x) { val = x; }
 * }
 */
class Solution {
    public ListNode addTwoNumbers(ListNode l1, ListNode l2) {
        Stack<Integer>stack1=new Stack<>();
        Stack<Integer>stack2=new Stack<>();
        while (l1!=null)
        {
            stack1.add(l1.val);
            l1=l1.next;
        }
        while (l2!=null)
        {
            stack2.add(l2.val);
            l2=l2.next;
        }
        ListNode val=new ListNode(0);//ଃ१ᕮᅩ
        int moreadd=0;//හ
        while (!stack1.isEmpty()||!stack2.isEmpty()||moreadd!=0)
        {
            int num=moreadd;//num=හ+᱾ᤒ1+᱾ᤒ2
            if(!stack1.isEmpty())
                num+=stack1.pop();
            if(!stack2.isEmpty())
                num+=stack2.pop();
            moreadd=num/10;
            num=num%10;
            //᱾ᤒ१ൊ
            ListNode node=new ListNode(num);
            node.next=val.next;
            val.next=node;
        }

class Solution {
    public int lastRemaining(int n, int m) {
        if(m==1)
            return n-1;
        List<Integer>list=new ArrayList<>();
        for(int i=0;i<n;i++)
        {
            list.add(i);
        }
        int index=0;
        while (list.size()>1)
        {
            index=(index+m-1)%(list.size());
            list.remove(index);
        }
        return list.get(0);
    }
}

f(n,m)=(f(n-1,m)+m)%n

f(10,3)=(f(9,3)+3)%10

f(1,3)=0
class Solution {
    int index=0;
    public int lastRemaining(int n, int m) {
         if(n==1)
            return 0;      
        return (lastRemaining(n-1,m)+m)%n;
    }
}

class Solution {
    public int lastRemaining(int n, int m) {
        int value=0;
            for(int i=1;i<=n;i++)
            {
                value=(value+m)%i;
            }
            return  value;
    }
}

public void  maopaosort(int[] a) {
  // TODO Auto-generated method stub
  for(int i=a.length-1;i>=0;i--)
  {
    for(int j=0;j<i;j++)
    {
      if(a[j]>a[j+1])
      {
        int team=a[j];
        a[j]=a[j+1];
        a[j+1]=team;
      }
    }
  }
}
public void quicksort(int [] a,int left,int right)
{
  int low=left;

  int high=right;
  //տԾኞහᕟ᩼ኴѺѺѺvery importantѺѺѺ
  if(low>high)//౼ྊ๵կ
    return;
  int k=a[low];//᷐क़ᑮᳵk᮷ྲਙय̶़
  while(low<high)//ੜԭa[low],य़ԭa[low]̶
  {
    while(low<high&&a[high]>=k)//ᒫӞӻੜԭkྊ
    {
      high--;
    }
    //ᒫӞӻྲਙੜጱԧ
    a[low]=a[high];//کlowᗝ
    while(low<high&&a[low]<=k)//ࣁlowᒫӞӻय़ԭk׆ݦکa[high]ᗝ
    {
      low++;
    }
    a[high]=a[low];     
  }
  a[low]=k;//လ࿢ԏ
  quicksort(a, left, low-1);
  quicksort(a, low+1, right);   
}
public void insertsort (int a[])
{
  int team=0;
  for(int i=1;i<a.length;i++)
  {
    System.out.println(Arrays.toString(a));
    team=a[i];
    for(int j=i-1;j>=0;j--)
    {
      if(a[j]>team)
      {
        a[j+1]=a[j];
        a[j]=team;  
      } 
      else {
        break;
      }
    }
  } 
}

public void shellsort (int a[])
{
  int d=a.length;
  int team=0;//ᰁ
  for(;d>=1;d/=2)//౮dᕟ
    for(int i=d;i<a.length;i++)//ݢܨ
{
      team=a[i];
      for(int j=i-d;j>=0;j-=d)
      {       
        if(a[j]>team)
        {
          a[j+d]=a[j];
          a[j]=team;  
        }
        else {
          break;
        }
      }
    } 
}

public void selectSort(int[] arr) {
  for (int i = 0; i < arr.length - 1; i++) {
    int min = i; // ᗝ
    for (int j = i + 1; j < arr.length; j++) {
      if (arr[j] < arr[min]) {
        min = j; // ᗝ
      }
    }
    if (min != i) {
      swap(arr, i, min); // ӨᒫiᗝᬰᤈԻഘ
    }
  }
}
private void swap(int[] arr, int i, int j) {
  int temp = arr[i];
  arr[i] = arr[j];
  arr[j] = temp;
}

ᔰԅ๋ੜ(๋य़)ᔰ҅ইຎᕳ਎ৼ᧣

ෙԻഘӥᑏ(shiftDown҂,ӧᬦᵱᥝဳ఺ྌ෸ෆӻञጱय़ੜ૪ᕪ

ӻ̶ᬯ໏॔๥ଶ੪ԅO(nlogn).௛ጱ෸ᳵ॔๥ଶԅO(n)+O(nlogn)=O(nlogn).
ਫሿդᎱԅғ
static void swap(int arr[],int m,int n)
{
  int team=arr[m];
  arr[m]=arr[n];
  arr[n]=team;
}
//ӥᑏԻഘ ഘ౮Ӟӻञ(ੜ໑)
static void shiftDown(int arr[],int index,int len)//0 ᗝӧአ
{
  int leftchild=index*2+1;//ૢ਎ৼ
  int rightchild=index*2+2;//਎ৼ
  if(leftchild>=len)
    return;
  else if(rightchild<len&&arr[rightchild]<arr[index]&&arr[rightchild]<arr[leftchild])//ݦ
ଚӬଫᧆԻഘ
  {
    swap(arr, index, rightchild);//Իഘᜓᅩ꧊
    shiftDown(arr, rightchild, len);//ӥ᯿຅
  }
  else if(arr[leftchild]<arr[index])//Իഘૢ਎ৼ
  {
    swap(arr, index, leftchild);
    shiftDown(arr, leftchild, len);
  }
}
//ୌ౮ञ
static void creatHeap(int arr[])
{
  for(int i=arr.length/2;i>=0;i--)
  {
    shiftDown(arr, i,arr.length);
  }
}
static void heapSort(int arr[])

  //step1ୌञ
  creatHeap(arr);

private static void mergesort(int[] array, int left, int right) {
  int mid=(left+right)/2;
  if(left<right)
  {
    mergesort(array, left, mid);
    mergesort(array, mid+1, right);
    merge(array, left,mid, right);
  }
}
private static void merge(int[] array, int l, int mid, int r) {
  int lindex=l;int rindex=mid+1;
  int team[]=new int[r-l+1];
  int teamindex=0;
  while (lindex<=mid&&rindex<=r) {//ଚ
    if(array[lindex]<=array[rindex])
    {
      team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
    }
    else {        
      team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
    }
  }
  while(lindex<=mid)//ݢܨے
{
    team[teamindex++]=array[lindex++];
  }
  while(rindex<=r)
  {
    team[teamindex++]=array[rindex++];
  } 
  for(int i=0;i<teamindex;i++)
  {
    array[l+i]=team[i];
  }
}

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
//ғbigsai
public class bucketSort {
  public static void main(String[] args) {
    int a[]= {1,8,7,44,42,46,38,34,33,17,15,16,27,28,24};
    List[] buckets=new ArrayList[5];
    for(int i=0;i<buckets.length;i++)//۸
    {
      buckets[i]=new ArrayList<Integer>();
    }
    for(int i=0;i<a.length;i++)//੒ଫ໲Ӿ
    {
      int index=a[i]/10;//ݩ
      buckets[index].add(a[i]);
    }
    for(int i=0;i<buckets.length;i++)//ᬰᤈഭଧ(አᔮᕹᛔଃளഭ)
    {
      buckets[i].sort(null);
      for(int j=0;j<buckets[i].size();j++)//ڊ
      {
        System.out.print(buckets[i].get(j)+" ");
      }
    } 
  }
}

public static void countSort(int a[])
{
  int min=Integer.MAX_VALUE;int max=Integer.MIN_VALUE;
  for(int i=0;i<a.length;i++)//کmax޾min
  {
    if(a[i]<min) 
      min=a[i];
    if(a[i]>max)
      max=a[i];
  }
  int count[]=new int[max-min+1];//ᔰᬰᤈᦇහ
  for(int i=0;i<a.length;i++)
  {
    count[a[i]-min]++;
  }
  //꧊
  int index=0;
  for(int i=0;i<count.length;i++)
  {
    while (count[i]-->0) {
      a[index++]=i+min;//ํmin಍ฎ፥ྋ꧊
    }
  }
}

static void radixSort(int[] arr)//int ࣳ ஃૢ
{
  List<Integer>bucket[]=new ArrayList[10];
  for(int i=0;i<10;i++)
  {
    bucket[i]=new ArrayList<Integer>();
  }
  //๋य़꧊
  int max=0;//ᦡ᮷ฎྋහ
  for(int i=0;i<arr.length;i++)
  {
    if(arr[i]>max)
      max=arr[i];
  }
  int divideNum=1;//1 10 100 100……ጱහਁ
  while (max>0) {//max ޾num ګ
    for(int num:arr)
    {
      bucket[(num/divideNum)%10].add(num);//ᯈ ੒ଫbucketӾ
    }
    divideNum*=10;
    max/=10;
    int idx=0;
    //තᵞ ᯿ෛഗ᩸හഝ
    for(List<Integer>list:bucket)
    {
      for(int num:list)
      {
        arr[idx++]=num;
      }
      list.clear();//አ
    }
  }
}
